### [145] [FailureSpot: Label-Efficient Timestamp-Level Failure Detection for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04277)
*Jie Ma,Zongxi Liu,Yi Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种高效的数据框架，用于细粒度、时间戳级别地检测视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的失效。通过无标签数据弱监督结合主动学习，提升检测准确率，并减少人工标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA机器人策略虽有潜力，但在长时任务中失效难以预测，存在安全风险。现有检测方法要么反应滞后，要么监督信号粗糙，导致精度和定位能力有限，需要一种更精准且高效的失效检测方法。

Method: 提出用无标签的VLA动作分段提取弱监督信号（如连续分段不一致、动作冻结、随机激进行为），结合主动学习策略，仅对最不确定的轨迹片段进行时间戳级人工标注，并基于这些高价值标签微调检测器。

Result: 在多种VLA策略上实验，所提方法显著提升了时间戳级和轨迹级的失效检测准确率，且减少了人工数据标注工作。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了更高效、更精确的VLA策略失效检测，为实际机器人部署中的安全性提供了支持。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies have shown strong potential for general-purpose robotic manipulation, but they can still fail unpredictably during long-horizon execution, making reliable failure detection essential for safe deployment. Existing methods either rely on visual models that typically detect failures only after erroneous actions have occurred, or use lightweight proactive detectors trained on VLA internal representations. However, these proactive methods are often supervised with trajectory-level labels, causing normal pre-failure behavior in unsuccessful trajectories to be incorrectly labeled as failure. This supervision mismatch introduces label noise and limits both trajectory-level detection accuracy and precise timestamp-level failure localization. In this work, we study fine-grained timestamp-level VLA failure detection while addressing the cost of dense annotation. We propose a data-efficient framework that first leverages unlabeled VLA action chunks to construct action-derived weak supervision signals, capturing abnormal patterns such as inconsistent consecutive chunks, frozen or idle actions, and aggressive random motions. We then use active learning to select only the most uncertain trajectories for timestamp-level annotation and fine-tune the detector with these informative labels. Experiments across multiple VLA policies show that our method improves both timestamp-level and trajectory-level failure detection performance.

</details>


### [146] [VLA-Precision: Asymmetric Co-Bootstrapping for Efficient Real-World Online RL of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04355)
*Chenyu Su,Zhaolong Shen,Yuan Qian,Chen Qian,Rui Zhang,Feng Yan,Weixing Chen,Fei Zhang,Jiamin Wang,Shuang Cong,Weiwei Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文推出了VLA-Precision，一种高效的现实世界在线强化学习框架，通过结构创新显著提升了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在高精度任务中的可靠性和执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大规模预训练视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）可以广泛操控现实世界，但它们在高精度和高重复性任务上表现不可靠。使用在线强化学习进一步提升VLA能力虽有潜力，但会面临策略漂移和运算/采样效率低下两大瓶颈。

Method: 提出Asymmetric Co-Bootstrapping（ACoB）算法，通过早期干预引导行为学习，加速策略提升并提升体验质量，同时通过回报传播和偏好排序逐步校准价值估计、抑制策略漂移。此外，提出ACoB-Stream架构，对VLA实现状态解耦与按需流式处理，大幅提升吞吐与计算效率。

Result: 在九个高精度化学任务和四种机器人上评测，VLA-Precision平均成功率高达98.3%，平均每个任务仅需45.8分钟，单集运行速度比现有VLA和RL基线快1.2倍和1.8倍。

Conclusion: ACoB与ACoB-Stream能够显著提升大规模VLA模型在现实世界高精度任务中的稳定性、效率和精度，有效推动高精度自主操作的发展。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models enable broad manipulation but remain unreliable in tasks demanding precision and repeatability. Applying real-world online reinforcement learning (RL) to VLA post-training enables autonomous trial-and-error improvement beyond demonstrations alone, but exposes two bottlenecks: 1) unreliable value signals can induce policy drift; 2) large-VLA overhead constrains throughput and sample efficiency. To address these challenges, we present VLA-Precision, an efficient real-world online RL framework featuring the Asymmetric Co-Bootstrapping (ACoB) algorithm and the ACoB-Stream architecture. Specifically, ACoB establishes asymmetric co-bootstrapping across timescales: early intervention-guided behavioral learning rapidly improves policy performance while enhancing online experience quality. As autonomous experience accumulates, global return propagation and local preference ranking progressively calibrate value estimates, yielding relative action advantages for reference-regularized policy improvement while suppressing drift. To enable ACoB on large VLAs, we develop ACoB-Stream, a closed-loop experience--policy architecture that establishes invariant-state decoupling and on-demand streaming as design principles, delivering up to 10.9$\times$ improvements in throughput and computational efficiency. Extensive evaluations on nine high-precision chemistry tasks across four categories and four robot embodiments show that VLA-Precision achieves 98.3\% mean success rate in 45.8 min/task, with 27.6 s episodes running at 1.2$\times$ and 1.8$\times$ the speeds of VLA and RL baselines. Resources are available at https://vla-precision.github.io.

</details>


### [147] [Scalable Edge-assisted Fusion and Path Prediction for Connected Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04364)
*Tyler Landle,Jackson Isenberg,Abhijit Chatterjee,Alexandros Daglis,Umakishore Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于路侧单元(RSU)的边缘计算方案Conductor，通过融合路侧和自动驾驶车辆(AV)的信息，构建统一世界模型并进行未来轨迹预测，从而提升自动驾驶规划效果，并确保信息时效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶车辆的规划算法依赖于本车传感器，但受视野遮挡和交通环境影响，感知范围有限。通过边缘计算设施(如RSU)融合多源信息，可全面提升交通流畅性和安全性。然而，相关信息需在严格的时效要求下送达车辆，现有的本地信息融合方式难以兼顾效果和可扩展性，因此亟需新的解决方案。

Method: 作者提出Conductor系统，采用RSU为锚点，在区域内融合AV和RSU信息，生成统一世界模型和轨迹预测。系统设计包含两大核心：1) 遮挡感知选择器优先选取覆盖盲区的车辆信息，2) 运行时控制器动态调整信息融合数量和轨迹预测量，以始终满足信息新鲜度(AoI)时限。

Result: 在仿真平台上评估表明，该选择器与控制器组合在多种交通场景下始终满足AoI安全限制，兼容最高31辆车，融合效果接近理想(Oracle)方案，显著优于随机选择方法。

Conclusion: Conductor通过智能选择和动态控制，有效融合多车与RSU信息，在不突破信息时效的前提下大幅提升自动驾驶规划信息的质量和安全性，对CAV系统的可扩展性和应用落地具有推动作用。

Abstract: The planning algorithms inside an Autonomous Vehicle (AV) rely on information from on-board sensors whose line of sight is limited by emerging traffic conditions and occlusions. Edge-assisted creation of a unified world model fusing information from AVs and Road Side Units (RSUs) in a geographical locale, and the prediction of AVs' future trajectories, can enhance the
  planning algorithms inside AVs to improve quality metrics, such as better traffic flow and collision prevention. AVs participating in such enhancements are called Connected Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs). However, such information generated by the edge (world model and motion predictions) must reach the planners within a tight Age of Information (AoI) time budget to be
  useful. The state of the art fuses per-CAV information: each AV fuses inputs from other actors locally, which limits both scalability with actor count and quality of results.
  We present Conductor, an edge-based solution for creating a unified world model from the perspective of a fixed anchor (e.g., an RSU) in a locale and predicting future trajectories of AVs in that locale. Our solution adheres to the AoI time budget by dynamically limiting the number of AVs that would lead to the best quality of results. Specifically, we introduce an
  occlusion-aware selector that favors information contribution by AVs that detect objects in the locale not covered by RSUs. We pair this selector with a runtime controller that adapts both the number of AV inputs to fuse and the amount of trajectory predictions in each cycle to stay within the AoI time budget. Evaluation on CAV simulation infrastructure shows our joint
  selector-controller meets the AoI safety bound across traffic scenarios with up to 31 CAVs, with fusion fidelity close to an Oracle and much better than a random selector under the same AoI constraint.

</details>


### [148] [AquaBEV: Monocular Underwater BEV Occupancy with 3D Sonar Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04411)
*Trung Tien Dong,Shengji Jin,Chen Chen,Yi Sheng,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出AquaBEV，它能通过单张水下RGB图像预测鸟瞰视角(BEV)占据情况，并在训练阶段引入声呐数据进行几何监督，在基准测试中表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人任务中，安全导航高度依赖于对环境空间的理解。仅凭RGB图像因几何信息有限难以精确预测BEV占据图，作者希望引入更强的几何监督以提升单目模型性能。

Method: 提出AquaBEV方法：利用配对的3D成像声呐数据作为训练监督，模型将视觉特征映射到免标定的极坐标表示空间，并沿距离维度做因果解码，最后重建为笛卡尔BEV坐标，用统一协议构建了新的水下RGB-声呐占据基准。

Result: AquaBEV在标准的RGB-声呐占据预测基准测试中实现了31.4的可见IoU和38.6的观测IoU，分别比最强基线方法提升4.0%和4.3%。

Conclusion: AquaBEV通过结合3D声呐监督与视觉信息，有效提升了单目水下BEV占据预测精度，为实际水下导航和监测提供了可靠新方案。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater robots are widely used for exploration, monitoring, and inspection, where safe navigation depends on understanding the surrounding free and occupied space. Bird's eye view (BEV) occupancy provides such a representation, but predicting it from a single underwater RGB image is difficult due to limited, unreliable geometric cues from appearance alone. 3D imaging sonar offers complementary geometric measurements to supervise this task.
  We introduce AquaBEV, a monocular underwater occupancy model that predicts local BEV occupancy from a single RGB image, using paired 3D imaging sonar as geometric supervision during training. AquaBEV maps visual features into a calibration free polar representation and applies causal decoding along the range dimension before reconstructing the prediction in Cartesian BEV coordinates. A controlled underwater occupancy benchmark was established, adapting representative occupancy methods to the same RGB to sonar task under a unified protocol. AquaBEV achieves 31.4 Visible IoU and 38.6 Observed IoU, 4.0% and 4.3% relative improvements over the strongest transferred baseline.

</details>


### [149] [Achieving Asymptotic Near-Optimality Without $δ$-Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04464)
*Michael Moncton,Eric Frew*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分析了采样式运动规划算法在逼近最优轨迹时的理论假设及其局限性，指出存在“crowding out”现象会破坏部分理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样-基运动规划算法因其能够高效处理高维复杂环境和动力学约束，在运动轨迹规划领域被广泛采用。许多相关研究声称，这类算法可渐近逼近最优解，但对其理论基础的严谨性存疑，有必要重新审视其成立前提。

Method: 作者分析了现有理论证明，指出这些证明隐含了δ-相似轨迹一旦采样即必然保留的假设。文中提出“crowding out”现象，并以特定环境与系统为例，展示该现象实际发生情况。

Result: 证明在存在“crowding out”时，采样到δ-相似轨迹段并不能保证其被算法保留，理论上的最优性渐近性假设不总成立。但作者也提出，即使没有δ-相似轨迹的保证，只要妥善考虑“crowding out”，算法仍可实现渐近近最优。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了采样-基运动规划理论中的隐含假设和实际问题，提醒后续算法设计者应关注“crowding out”效应，并在理论证明时充分考虑实际采样保留机制，以确保结果的正确性和实用性。

Abstract: Sampling-based motion planning algorithms are a popular class of trajectory planning algorithm due to their speed in complex, high-dimensional environments and ability to handle kinodynamic constraints, specifically through the use of forward dynamics propagation. Many such planners claim to achieve asymptotic near-optimality by proving the almost sure sampling of trajectories that are close to an optimal trajectory in the state space, known as $δ$-similar trajectories. This paper shows that the proof behind asymptotic $δ$-similarity relies on an unstated assumption that $δ$-similar trajectory segments will always be kept once sampled. This assumption does not hold in general. A problematic case, referred to as ``crowding out,'' is described, where locally low-cost paths prevent trajectories that are $δ$-similar to the optimal trajectory from being added to the tree. It is shown, however, that asymptotic near-optimality guarantees can still be achieved without guarantees of $δ$-similar solution trajectories when crowding out is properly accounted for. An example environment and system are provided where crowding out is shown to occur, demonstrating a scenario where inductively sampling a $δ$-similar solution trajectory is impossible.

</details>


### [150] [SocioGesture: Real-Time and Adaptive Social Gesture Perception for Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04545)
*Wenjin Fu,Li-Fan Wu,Jerin Peter,Chip Huyen,Boyuan Chen,Jan Liphardt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了SocioGesture系统，实现了机器人对社交手势的实时、低延迟识别，并在部分遮挡等复杂情况下表现出强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人与人互动需要识别复杂的社交信号（如邀请、拒绝等），而不仅仅是明确指令。实际部署中，这些信号常处于遮挡、视角变化和硬件延迟下，导致感知噪声大、识别难度高。

Method: 提出SocioGesture系统，利用紧凑的置信度骨架表达和轻量双流模型，将身体运动与手部动作融合，适配低延迟感知。训练过程中采用遮挡感知骨架扰动，提升对身体部位缺失和运动不稳定的鲁棒性。部署时对不确定片段进行离线标注和自适应扩展词汇。

Result: 在混合室内外HRI情景采集的数据集上，SocioGesture对隔离测试主体有很好的识别能力，在关节点遮挡下明显优于其他方法，并可实时运行在机器人端侧设备。

Conclusion: SocioGesture为交互机器人实现了兼顾鲁棒性、效率和自适应的社交感知能力，推动了实际应用进展。

Abstract: Robots interacting with people must recognize not only explicit commands, but also social cues such as invitations, refusals, and unavailability. In real deployments, these cues must be inferred from noisy onboard perception under partial occlusion, changing viewpoints, and strict latency constraints. We present SocioGesture, a real-time adaptive social gesture perception system for human-robot interaction (HRI). SocioGesture uses a compact confidence-aware body-hand skeleton representation and a lightweight dual-stream model that fuses body motion with hand articulation for low-latency onboard recognition. To improve deployment robustness, we train the model with occlusion-aware skeleton corruption, exposing it to missing hands, occluded arms, and temporally unstable keypoints without increasing the inference cost. On a social gesture dataset collected in mixed indoor-outdoor HRI scenarios, SocioGesture achieves strong held-out-subject recognition, substantially improves robustness under structured joint occlusion, and runs in real time on a robot-mounted edge device. During deployment, uncertain interaction segments are saved for offline labeling and adaptation, enabling SocioGesture to expand its gesture vocabulary while preserving performance in the original classes. These results demonstrate a practical path toward robust, efficient, and adaptive social perception for interactive robots.

</details>


### [151] [Continual Field-Adaptive Models (CFAMs) for Post-Deployment Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04552)
*Amarjot Singh,Tanmay R. Pancholi,Jainam Kothari,Shrirang Mahajan,Ketan Bansal,Zackory Erickson,Giuseppe Loianno,Alexandre M. Bayen,Jeff Schneider,Vince Nakayama*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CFAM（Continual Field-Adaptive Models）的模型，能够实现机器在任务关键操作中的自主学习和持续适应，提升了在物理平台上的少样本技能学习和新场景自适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关键任务操作中，让机器自主代替人类在危险环境中完成任务是非常重要的能力。然而，这些场景通常缺乏充足的训练数据，且只能依赖本地计算资源，面对新情况时还要保持原有能力不被遗忘。因此，需要一种高效、持续自适应的自主学习方法。

Method: 提出了CFAM模型，包括一个冻结的慢学习组件和一个可快速适应的Capsule Field。慢学习组件分别负责多模态传感数据到三维几何的映射、任务分解与评估以及动作执行；Capsule Field基于‘Competence Capsules’实现无梯度、一次性快速学习。技能通过实验室少样本学习安装，并可以在实际环境中持续更新。

Result: 在五种机器平台（机械臂、四足机器人、人形机器人、无人机和越野车）上进行了评估。与基线方法相比，CFAM在达到标准策略性能时所需数据量减少为40%（即2.5倍节约），并且在测试时对接近新颖任务的自主适应使执行成功率提升了13.9个百分点。在连续任务中，保持原有能力的能力（backward transfer）大大优于对比方法（-0.5 vs -11.4）。

Conclusion: CFAM实现了低样本技能学习、基于验证的野外自主适应以及对以往技能的有效保留。对于实际部署的自主系统，CFAM为后续持续自适应提供了有界范围的物理智能。

Abstract: Unattended interactive autonomy - machines that step into danger in place of humans and complete tasks with human tools - remains a missing capability in mission-critical operations. These domains offer scarce training data and only onboard compute, yet deployed systems must face novelty without erasing prior competence.
  We introduce Continual Field-Adaptive Models (CFAMs), which learn efficiently in the lab and continue learning after deployment through autonomous, gradient-free, on-device updates. CFAM uses a complementary learning architecture with a frozen slow-learning component and a fast-learning Capsule Field. The slow component contains three cortices: Sensor, which maps multimodal input into 3D-grounded geometry; Reasoning, which decomposes tasks into skills and evaluates outcomes; and Action, which executes geometric skills. The Capsule Field stores field learning one-shot and gradient-free as Competence Capsules. Skill installation is few-shot in the lab and continual in the field; open-world novelty is outside scope.
  We evaluate CFAM across five embodiments: manipulator, quadruped, humanoid, quadrotor, and off-road vehicle. Baselines (pi0, CogACT, SpatialVLA) use the same in-house multi-embodiment dataset for physical-platform comparisons. CFAM reaches the operating point of a standard policy trained on the full prior-training dataset using 40% of the data, or 2.5x fewer trajectories. At test time, autonomous capture of verified near-OOD cases improves action success by 13.9 percentage points. In sequential simulation, backward transfer is -0.5 percentage points versus -11.4 for LoRA. CFAM therefore provides a bounded form of post-deployment physical intelligence: few-shot skill learning, autonomous field growth from verified near-OOD experience, and retention of prior competence.

</details>


### [152] [NavArena: Automated Construction of Goal-Oriented Navigation Benchmarks from 3D Gaussian Splatting Reconstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04602)
*Junhui Wang,Wei Yang,Xinyao Li,Ningjing Fan,Yuehao Yin,Xuecheng Chen,Chao Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NavArena框架，将固定3D高斯斑点(3DGS)重建转换为可用于视觉导航评测的基准，并自动生成数百万条专家轨迹，实现大规模、可复现的导航评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3DGS重建虽然能够生成高质量的新视角，但缺乏可行性约束、有效目标以及用于导航评测的闭环协议，限制了其在真实导航任务中的应用。

Method: NavArena框架包括：1）使用冻结的3DGS模型实现自我中心化RGB-D渲染；2）基于高斯密度和高度统计生成占用代价图，用于可达性与碰撞查询；3）利用多视角开放词汇语义分割自动提取导航目标。这些组成部分支持导航任务的自动生成与统一闭环评测。

Result: 在超过2000个场景中，NavArena自动生成了2,220万条专家轨迹。作者通过空间与语义层面评价导航表示，并通过策略滚动验证协议的诊断价值。

Conclusion: NavArena使得基于大规模3DGS重建的导航评测变得可扩展且可复现。所有的基准生成工具、评测协议和衍生数据都将公开发布。

Abstract: Fixed 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) reconstructions provide realistic novel views but lack the traversability constraints, valid goals, and closed-loop protocols required for navigation evaluation. We introduce NavArena, an automated framework that transforms fixed 3DGS reconstructions into benchmarks for goal-oriented visual navigation. NavArena integrates a frozen 3DGS model for egocentric RGB-D rendering, an occupancy costmap derived from Gaussian density and height statistics for reachability and collision queries, and semantic goal candidates lifted from multi-view open-vocabulary masks. These components support the automatic generation and unified closed-loop evaluation of goal-oriented navigation episodes. Across more than 2{,}000 scenes, NavArena generates 22.2 million expert trajectories. Spatial and semantic evaluations assess the derived navigation representations, while policy rollouts demonstrate the diagnostic value of the unified evaluation protocol. NavArena enables scalable and reproducible navigation evaluation on large-scale 3DGS reconstructions, and all benchmark-generation tools, evaluation protocols, and derived assets will be released publicly.

</details>


### [153] [Open-Set 3D Scene Graphs for Field Robotics: An Outdoor Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04607)
*Chad R. Samuelson,Gabriel R. Slade,Joshua G. Mangelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文系统性分析了室外三维场景图（3DSGs）及其与视觉-语言模型（VLMs）结合时在实际环境下的表现，指出了其导航、语义一致性及区域理解方面的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DSGs近年来在高层机器人推理与地图构建领域广受关注，但其在复杂、动态的户外环境中的实际表现和局限性尚不明晰，尤其是在引入开放式视觉-语言模型后，其鲁棒性和泛化能力有待系统实证。

Method: 作者选择5个户外机器人数据集，并以Terra 3DSG为案例，系统评估了语义点嵌入、基于节点的导航、区域理解及内存占用等核心组件。同时引入了新的一致性度量指标，用于评估3DSG在环境重复遍历下的语义和结构稳定性。

Result: VLM点嵌入中异常值及多模态普遍存在，30%的点中异常值占比超过0.1。3DSG在基于导航的目标检索任务中达到近70%的成功率，但路径效率存在损失（平均66%最优路径效率）。区域理解任务表现仍低迷（平均F1为0.359）。空间占用较小，多公里环境场景小于600MB。

Conclusion: 户外3DSG能在大规模复杂环境下维持紧凑并相对一致的语义结构，但多模态处理、融合可通行性信息及提升区域级理解仍是亟需突破的难题。

Abstract: Three-dimensional scene graphs (3DSGs) have emerged as a promising approach for building geometrically grounded, semantically informed, hierarchical general-purpose maps to support high-level robotic reasoning. However, the behavior of 3DSGs in real-world outdoor deployments remains poorly understood, particularly when combined with open-set vision-language models (VLMs). In this field report, we analyze the components common to most 3DSG representations across five outdoor robotic datasets to characterize challenges that arise in complex outdoor environments. Using the recently proposed Terra 3DSG as a case study, we investigate semantic point embeddings, place-node graph navigation, region-level understanding, and memory size across the five diverse datasets. We additionally introduce novel consistency metrics to evaluate whether semantic and structural graph properties remain stable across repeated traversals of the same environment. Our analysis reveals that outliers and multiple modes are common in VLM point embeddings across all tested datasets with outlier ratios above $0.1$ for around $30\%$ of points. We demonstrate the feasibility of outdoor 3DSGs for navigation-based object retrieval, achieving success rates near $70\%$, though performance is limited by traversability failures and inefficient routing, with trajectories averaging approximately $66\%$ suboptimal path efficiency. Region-level understanding remains challenging in complex natural environments, with low average F1 scores around $0.359$. Overall, our results show that outdoor 3DSGs can maintain compact (less than $600$MB for multi-kilometer trajectories) and relatively consistent large-scale environment representations, while highlighting open challenges in handling multiple semantic modes, incorporating traversability into graph structures, and improving higher-level region understanding.

</details>


### [154] [Pack It My Way: Triadic Human-Robot Collaboration for Personalized Autonomous Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04620)
*Sandeep Chowdary Kotapati,Yanxin Gao,Tsung-Chi Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文研究了在个性化自主包装中，语音代理是否可以替代人类专家进行用户偏好调节，从而减少对专家的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在个人化包装任务中，机器人需要适应用户难以仅凭场景几何推断出的个性化偏好，以往均依赖人类专家高频介入，影响了实际部署的可扩展性。

Method: 提出“住户-纠错中介-机器人”三方协作模型，将语音代理与人类专家作为中介进行对比，并通过Show-Correct-Generalize流程对三类包装任务开展用户研究，评估偏好修正和泛化能力。

Result: 语音代理在三类偏好中有两类取得了与人类专家类似的效果，即便指令更简短且信息较少；在易用性上两者接近，但人类专家被认为更可靠。

Conclusion: 语音代理有望减少对人类专家的需求，但在可靠性与偏好泛化等方面仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Personalized autonomous packing requires robots to account for resident preferences that cannot be inferred from scene geometry alone. Expert teleoperators can interpret these preferences and translate them into feasible robot actions, but continuous expert involvement limits scalable deployment. In this paper, we investigate triadic human-robot collaboration among a resident, a correction mediator, and a robot by comparing human-expert and voice-agent mediation. We evaluate the two conditions in a user study across Protection, Compactness, andGrouping tasks, using a Show-Correct-Generalize process to assess preference correction and subsequent generalization after the surrounding objects are rearranged. Results show that voice-agent mediation achieves outcomes comparable to human-expert mediation in two of the three preference categories, despite receiving shorter and less detailed instructions. Both mediators are similarly easy to use, although the human expert is perceived as more reliable. These findings demonstrate the potential of voice agents to reduce expert involvement while identifying perceived reliability and preference generalization as remaining challenges.

</details>


### [155] [Dressing in Motion: A Human Motion-Aware Diffusion Policy for Robot-Assisted Dressing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04759)
*Haoxiang Sun,Fangyuan Wang,Songhao Huang,Justina Y. W. Liu,Jihong Zhu,Peng Zhou,David Navarro-Alarcon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种基于视觉-运动策略的机器人辅助穿衣方法，能够高效适应动态的用户手臂运动，在模拟和真实场景中均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对老年人及行动障碍者日常穿衣困难，但现有机器人在应对动态人体动作时受限于服装与人体复杂接触及遮挡，难以实现动作同步。

Method: 提出了一种基于扩散策略（Diffusion Policy）的视觉-运动策略，从静态专家演示中学习穿衣技能，并能泛化到动态手臂运动场景。该方法利用基于偏微分方程（PDE）扩散的面向目标的表征，捕捉手臂轴向分布，通过采样和配准运动相关区域，实现对手臂运动的近似及执行轨迹的自适应调整。

Result: 在包含九名参与者、三种服装、六种手臂运动模式的仿真与实人实验中，该方法在穿衣进度、运动自由度及用户舒适度上均超过对照方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法可实现更自然、舒适且高效的机器人辅助自适应穿衣，对提升辅助机器人的实际应用能力具有重要意义。

Abstract: Robotic dressing assistance is a promising solution for supporting older adults with physical impairments in daily living. However, dressing under human motion remains challenging, as complex garment--human contact and occlusions make it difficult to generate actions aligned with arm movements. In this letter, we propose a visuomotor policy that learns dressing skills from static expert demonstrations and generalizes to dynamic user-motion scenarios. A diffusion policy tailored to garment--human interaction geometry learns from partially observed point clouds with varied arm postures. We then introduce an object-centric representation based on PDE diffusion to capture the axial distribution of the arm. By sampling motion-relevant regions and registering them across consecutive observations, the proposed method approximates arm motion and reactively adapts the executed trajectory. We evaluate our method in simulation and a real-world human study involving nine participants, three garment types, and six arm-motion patterns. Results show that our method outperforms baselines in dressing progress, freedom of movement, and user comfort. The project website is https://anonymous.4open.science/w/dressing-in-motion.

</details>


### [156] [Continuous Cognitive Coverage for Autonomous Robots via Event-Dependent Cognitive Treatment and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04770)
*Hong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向自主机器人事件的连续认知覆盖框架，实现了对所有认知事件进行差异化、持续的认知处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的机器人认知系统多为任务驱动或反应性，仅对部分事件经过预定义程序处理，无法全面、灵活地响应连贯环境输入。机器人在动态环境中需对每一认知事件进行合适处理，以支持持续自主性。

Method: 提出连续认知覆盖框架，对每个被认知采纳的事件，根据其状态、上下文和历史指定差异化认知处理，包括自动描述、记忆、风险预测、规划、类比等。熟悉事件自动处理，不熟悉事件则引入显式推理或回退机制。支持多认知过程的挂起、恢复和交错执行，并持续学习以完善事件处理方式。

Result: 实验结果显示：结构化处理准确率达96.76%，自动处理比例达93.66%；在突发延迟负载下认知覆盖率92.64%；持续学习联合准确率79.53%；新颖事件复用时100%实现自动处理。

Conclusion: 该框架显著提升了自主机器人对环境事件的连续、全面认知能力，实现了高自动化处理、高覆盖率及持续适应能力，有助于推动自主系统认知智能的进步。

Abstract: Autonomous robots continuously encounter objects, changes, and situations, and every event admitted into cognition should receive an appropriate cognitive treatment rather than remain untreated until an explicit task requires attention. However, existing task-driven, reactive, or fixed-reasoning approaches generally process only selected events or apply predefined reasoning procedures, making it difficult to provide continuous cognitive coverage with differentiated treatment. This paper proposes a continuous cognitive coverage framework in which every cognitively admitted event is assigned an event-dependent cognitive treatment according to its state, context, and history. Different events may therefore invoke description, memory, risk prediction, planning, diagnosis, analogy, or other learned treatments. Familiar events can be processed automatically by learned mechanisms, whereas unfamiliar or uncertain events invoke explicit deliberation or fallback reasoning. Multiple cognitive processes can be suspended, resumed, and interleaved so that cognitive processing continues as new events arrive or existing events await evidence. Validated experiences are continuously learned to automate, refine, and revise event-specific treatments. Experiments achieve 96.76% structured treatment accuracy with 93.66% automatic processing, 92.64% cognitive coverage under bursty-delayed workloads, and 79.53% continual-learning joint accuracy, with novel-event reuse reaching 100% automatic processing.

</details>


### [157] [HaptiNet: Networked Haptic Robots Enable Physical Co-presence in Geographically-Unconstrained Rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04799)
*Chenyang Sun,Mingjie Dong,Haodong Deng,Yudong Liu,Yi-Feng Chen,Jun Lin,Changlong Huang,Jie Guo,Yantong Liu,Yang Liu,Yuzhou Lin,Jianjun Long,Zheng Xing,Sining Zhao,Xuemin Zhang,Zhiyong Wang,Zhenhong Li,Dongrui Wu,Honghai Liu,Jian S. Dai,Mingming Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HaptiNet的网络化触觉机器人系统，使地理上分散的用户之间能够通过力反馈实现远程物理共在的合作康复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统合作式康复能提高参与度和任务表现，但必须依赖物理共在，不便于远距离、行动不便或资源匮乏的病人。远程康复虽能扩大覆盖，但当前方案缺乏真实触觉交互，用户体验受限。

Method: 设计并实现了一套低惯量、大行程、高力反馈能力的上肢触觉终端，并通过模仿学习增强的延迟补偿器，将多个终端连成分布式触觉网络，实现远程力互动。通过284名健康人与111名神经障碍患者，在实验室、跨城市和临床等多种场景下进行了验证。

Result: HaptiNet在单人和多人场景均能保持任务力反馈一致性。相比于单人和纯视觉合作训练，触觉协作可分别提升任务表现24%和22%，并显著增加参与度和人际运动同步性。在三地总计约4000公里的远程应用中，残障患者的训练表现提升3.87倍，努力度提升106%。

Conclusion: HaptiNet有效打破地理限制，实现远程真实触觉协作康复，提升了康复效果和患者参与度，有望成为未来远程康复的重要技术方案。

Abstract: Cooperative rehabilitation enhances engagement, task performance, and social-motor interaction, yet it demands physical co-presence: users must transmit forces, coordinate movements, and infer intent through haptic contact. Telerehabilitation promises to expand access for patients constrained by distance, mobility, or clinical disparities, yet current techniques remain predominantly audiovisual while leaving users haptically and physically isolated. Here, we introduce HaptiNet, a networked haptic robotic system enabling physical co-presence for geographically distributed users via force-mediated interaction. Each robotic terminal features a low-inertia, long-stroke design with high force-feedback capacity, tailored for haptic rendering in upper-limb training. Building on these terminals, HaptiNet creates a distributed haptic network with an imitation-learning-based delay compensator, enabling users to physically perceive and coordinate with one another over distance. We validated HaptiNet in 284 healthy participants and 111 patients with neurological impairments across progressively realistic settings, including laboratory tests, cross-city deployments, and clinical applications. HaptiNet preserved task-level force rendering consistency across single-user and multi-user scenarios. Compared with solo and visual cooperative training, haptic cooperation improved task performance by 24% and 22%, respectively, while also boosting engagement and interpersonal motor synchrony. Across three intercity links totaling approximately 4,000 km, HaptiNet maintained stable haptic interaction among patients with neurological impairments, producing a 3.87-fold greater baseline-to-training score improvement and a 106% higher patient-applied effort over the solo condition.

</details>


### [158] [CoLMIN: LLM-based Multi-Decision Path Negotiation for Cooperative Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04807)
*Zhe Huang,Zhaoxin Fan,Shuo Wang,Wenjun Wu,Xuan Zhao,Min Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的多车协同自主驾驶决策框架CoLMIN，通过多决策路径协商和反思机制，大幅提升了协同决策的质量与稳定性，在仿真中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多车协同驾驶依赖信息共享和协商机制，但由于复杂场景下决策空间的多样性，传统方法容易陷入次优且不稳定的决策，影响实际部署和安全性。

Method: CoLMIN框架包括三大模块：1）基于LLM的多意图协商模块（LMin），通过Negotiator-Evaluator范式生成多个驾驶意图，并共同评估；2）基于评估的浅反思模块（ESRM），分析协商结果并给予反馈，指导后续决策，加快共识形成；3）基于LLM的深反思模块（LDRM），对协商历史进行长期反思，减少认知固化，避免陷入次优解。

Result: 在CARLA仿真环境中的复杂交互驾驶场景下，CoLMIN框架的决策共识质量和整体性能显著超越现有协同驾驶方法。

Conclusion: 多路径、反思性协同决策可显著改善多车协同自动驾驶的实际表现，所提出CoLMIN框架证明了LLM在提升自动驾驶智能协作中的潜力和应用价值。

Abstract: Multi-vehicle cooperative autonomous driving enhances the safety and reliability of autonomous driving systems through information sharing among connected vehicles, demonstrating significant potential for improving traffic safety. LLM-based approaches leverage strong reasoning capabilities of LLMs to enable effective inter-vehicle negotiation and improve cooperative driving performance. However, driving decisions in complex traffic scenarios are inherently multi-solution in nature. As a result, existing negotiation-based methods often converge prematurely to suboptimal solutions, hindering consensus formation and limiting the practical deployment of cooperative autonomous driving systems. To address this challenge, we propose CoLMIN, the LLM-based multi-decision path negotiation framework for cooperative autonomous driving, achieving stable decision consensus through multi-decision path negotiation and reflective reasoning. To achieve stable and high-quality consensus in cooperative autonomous driving, CoLMIN consists of three key components: (i) an LLM-based Multi-Intent Negotiation module (LMin), which adopts a Negotiator-Evaluator paradigm and generates multiple candidate driving intentions for joint evaluation; (ii) an Evaluation-based Shallow Reflection Module (ESRM), which analyzes negotiation outcomes and provides feedback to guide subsequent negotiations, thereby accelerating consensus formation; and (iii) an LLM-based Deep Reflection Module (LDRM), which performs long-term reflection over negotiation histories to mitigate cognitive fixation and prevent the system from converging to suboptimal solutions. Experimental results in the CARLA simulation environment demonstrate that CoLMIN significantly outperforms existing methods in challenging interactive driving scenarios.

</details>


### [159] [Strict Modes Everywhere - Bringing Order Into Dynamics of Mechanical Systems by a Potential Compatible With the Geodesic Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04817)
*Arne Sachtler,Alin Albu-Schäffer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过设计特殊势能，使严格的非线性正则模态（NNM）能够遍布系统的全部构型空间，并通过弹性元件实现高效周期运动的方法。并设计了相应的控制器，实验在双自由度机械臂上取得良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严格的非线性正则模态在保守机械系统中运动非常规律，但一般只能形成孤立的轨迹族，限制了其应用和能量高效运动的实现。如何构建能够覆盖全部构型空间的严格NNM，为系统提供丰富高效的周期运动形式是一个重要问题。

Method: 设计一种特殊的势能函数，使系统的每个构型都属于唯一一个严格NNM，并且NNM都通过系统平衡点。此势能可用非线性弹性元件实现。提出两类控制器：一类用于稳定系统到目标NNM，另一类用于摆起与能量补偿。方法通过在具有两个自由度的机械臂上的实验进行验证。

Result: 通过双自由度机械臂实验，验证了设计势能填满构型空间的NNM方法的有效性，系统主要扭矩由弹性元件提供，节省执行器能耗，控制器实现了稳定和能量补偿，周期运动表现良好。

Conclusion: 提出的势能与弹性元件设计方法能够极大扩展严格非线性正则模态的适用范围，使系统可以高效执行多样化周期运动，验证实验显示该策略效果显著。

Abstract: Strict nonlinear normal modes provide very regular families of oscillations within conservative mechanical systems. However, a strict normal mode will generally be an isolated curve within the configuration space of the system. In this letter, we design a potential that will densely fill the configuration space with strict normal modes such that each configuration belongs to one mode and each mode passes through a common point, the equilibrium. As the potential can be realized by (nonlinear) elastic elements it can be used to execute a variety of periodic trajectories very efficiently. Most of the required torques will come from the elastic elements in the system and not from the actuators. We also design a controller stabilizing the system to a desired target mode and a controller performing swing-up and compensating dissipated energy. Finally, we showcase the approach for a two DoF manipulator. The experiments show that the approach performed well for the example system.

</details>


### [160] [Coupled Control and Wireless World Models for Resilient Remote Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04851)
*H. P. Madushanka,Sumudu Samarakoon,Mehdi Bennis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种面向通信弹性的远程机器人控制框架，通过联合学习机器人动力学和无线信道演化，实现能量高效与稳健的远程控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程机器人系统在有限通信资源、变化的信道条件以及环境干扰下难以保证可靠控制，传统方法高频传输大量感知数据导致通信、能耗和鲁棒性问题。

Method: 提出一种基于JEPA（Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture）世界模型的联合控制与无线建模方法，结合视觉观测和频谱、持久性图像RF特征，预测机器人状态和无线信道演化，据此实现自适应通信调度，并引入自适应弹性机制及时调整感知嵌入以应对环境变化。

Result: 在ROS-Gazebo-Sionna 机器人-无线联合仿真环境中，面对多种无线和视觉扰动，该框架在通信效率、鲁棒性和弹性方面均显著优于传统PID、无模型DQN和ViT-based预测方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够在减少上行通信的同时，保障远程机器人导航任务的可靠性，对远程智能体在无线环境下的高效稳健控制具有重要意义。

Abstract: Remote robotic systems operating over wireless networks must maintain reliable control despite limited communication resources, changing channel conditions, and environmental disturbances.However, continuously transmitting high-dimensional sensory observations, such as camera images, increases communication overhead and energy consumption while reducing robustness under unreliable connectivity.To address these challenges, this paper proposes a resilient communication-aware remote robotic control framework based on coupled control and wireless Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) world models that jointly capture robot dynamics and wireless channel evolution from visual observations and a combination of raw and structured radio frequency (RF) representations based on spectrograms and Persistence Images(PIs).The learned latent representations enable predictive communication scheduling by jointly forecasting future robot states and wireless conditions, thereby reducing unnecessary uplink transmissions while maintaining reliable control performance.Furthermore, an adaptive resilience mechanism detects latent prediction discrepancies and efficiently adapts perception embeddings to accommodate wireless and visual environmental changes without retraining the complete control policy.The proposed framework is evaluated in a synchronized Gazebo-Robot Operating System (ROS)-Sionna robot-wireless simulation environment under diverse wireless propagation and perception perturbations.Experimental results demonstrate significant improvements in communication efficiency, robustness, and resilience while maintaining navigation performance compared with conventional Proportional Integral Derivative (PID), model-free Deep Q-Network (DQN), and predictive approaches based on Vision Transformers(ViTs).

</details>


### [161] [Reasoning Without Inference Cost: Latent Semantic Scaffolding for Robot VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04893)
*Andrew Ting Yan Li,Zhuo Li,Zhelin Yang,Zhipeng Dong,Quentin Rouxel,Fei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的训练方法Latent Semantic Scaffolding（LSS），通过在训练阶段对Vision-Language-Action（VLA）模型进行物理推理动机的对齐，提高模型泛化和推理能力，且部署时没有额外推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型在训练时能学会“做什么”，但缺乏“为什么”的因果推理能力。尽管现有的推理机制提高了操作能力，但却需要推理时动态生成推理内容或状态预测，推理成本高且易积累。作者希望找到一种只在训练阶段引入推理、部署时不增加成本的方法。

Method: 提出Latent Semantic Scaffolding（LSS）训练方法：在VLA模型的模仿学习阶段引入辅助损失，将每个动作token的表示通过小型投影头与人类解释的物理推理文本表征对齐。训练时存在投影头，推理部署时去除，推理成本不增加。同时比较了两种对齐粒度：细粒度的Dense LSS（每个动作token与其操作阶段推理对齐）和粗粒度的Pooled LSS（整个episode对齐一次）。

Result: Dense LSS（细粒度对齐）在分布内任务成功率和转移至未见任务两方面表现最佳，而Pooled LSS（粗粒度）容易过拟合训练任务。表征分析显示Dense LSS促进了模型中操作阶段的表示区分，有利于泛化。

Conclusion: 通过在训练期间引入与物理推理文本的细粒度对齐，不仅提升了VLA模型在已知和未知任务下的泛化能力，还实现了推理阶段零附加成本，提供了一种高效提升因果推理能力的实用方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are trained by imitation and capture what action to take but not why; adding causal reasoning improves manipulation, but current methods pay for it at inference time - generating reasoning tokens or rolling out predicted future states at every step, a cost that compounds over long horizons. We ask whether this benefit can instead be captured during training and discarded before deployment. We introduce Latent Semantic Scaffolding (LSS), an auxiliary loss applied during human-demonstration pretraining that aligns a VLA's action-token representations to text embeddings of physical-reasoning rationales through a small projection head. The head is dropped at inference, leaving the unmodified base policy with zero added cost. Our central finding concerns alignment granularity: aligning each action token to the rationale of its own manipulation phase (Dense LSS) rather than to a single pooled episode-level embedding (Pooled LSS) yields representations that transfer markedly better to held-out tasks. Dense LSS attains both the best in-distribution success and the best transfer to tasks unseen during alignment, whereas pooled alignment over-specializes to the training task. A representational probe shows Dense LSS induces roughly twice the per-phase separability in the backbone, supporting that phase-local alignment is the operative mechanism.

</details>


### [162] [ToPos: Automated Optimal Positioning on Topographic Manifolds using Constrained Geodesic Voronoi Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05084)
*Rajesh Raveendran,Akseli Vanhamaa,Jaakko Suutala,Antti Tikanmäki,Juha Röning*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ToPos，一种面向地形的空间参考点（SRS）最优分布自动化框架，通过地表几何上的测地距离和优化算法，有效提升了复杂地形下的空间均衡采样与布点效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高崎岖地形中，传统2D欧氏方法易忽视地形起伏和障碍，导致空间点集分布失衡、重要区域难及，对自动化测绘、环境采样等任务造成不利影响。亟需发展兼顾地形几何与可达性的空间采样方法。

Method: 该方法将地形视为嵌入3D空间的离散二维流形，采用非欧测地距离替代平面距离。以约束测地Voronoi分解将点分配建模为优化问题，并用Riemannian加速梯度方法求解，同时限定可达安全区域，规避陡坡、植被、遮挡等不可通行区。

Result: 在非凸正弦型地形上的评估显示，ToPos利用测地度量有效减少了测绘投影失真，Voronoi单元面积的变异系数CV改进达约74%。

Conclusion: ToPos为GIS等场景提供了一套兼容真实地形、能显著提升表面采样均衡和点分布安全性的自动化框架，适用于3D测绘、环境采样、基础设施部署等应用。

Abstract: Reliable autonomous mapping, environmental sampling, last-mile logistics, and infrastructure deployment depend on the optimal surface area-balanced distribution of Spatial Reference Sites (SRS). Conventional 2D Euclidean methods often fail in high-relief environments by neglecting topographic variations and physical obstructions. This leads to significant planimetric distortion, spatial clustering, and the placement of targets in inaccessible or shadowed regions, compromising both data integrity and operational safety. This paper introduces ToPos, an automated framework for TOPography-aware Optimal Sampling on topographic manifolds. We treat the terrain as a discrete 2-dimensional manifold embedded in 3D Euclidean space and replace standard flat-map distances with non-Euclidean geodesic distances that follow the actual surface geometry. The point distribution is formulated as an optimization problem using a Constrained Geodesic Voronoi Decomposition, solved via a Riemannian Nesterov Accelerated Gradient (NAG) engine. Our approach restricts target locations to a feasible "safe zone," accounting for non-traversable slopes, vegetation, environmental occlusions, etc. Through evaluations on non-convex sinusoidal manifolds, we show that ToPos mitigates planimetric distortion by utilizing geodesic metrics. This approach results in a $\sim$74% improvement in optimal surface area-balanced distribution, as measured by the coefficient of variation (CV) of the Voronoi cell areas. The framework is architected as a Geographic Information System (GIS)-ready micro-service to bolster the mentioned applications.
  Index Terms: Topographic Manifolds, Geodesic Voronoi Decomposition, Infrastructure Deployment, 3D Mapping, Spatial Sampling, and Non-Euclidean Optimization.

</details>


### [163] [A Schema Bounded Language Model for Refining Robot Policies Without Destabilizing Local Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05133)
*Chongwen Dong,Mithun Paul Saint-Germain,Pinjari Asif,Carlo R. daCunha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在去中心化系统中，由不同大型语言模型(LLM)驱动的多机器人协同导航场景，各机器人独立结合LLM策略、UCB算法及Double DQN控制器，无统一中央规划。实验显示最完整配置方案效率最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体多样化和系统去中心化需求增长，异构、多智能体系统需解决如何高效协作与决策，特别是各自由不同LLM驱动且无中心控制时，提升团队导航表现成为关键。

Method: 采用NetLogo--Python平台，三台机器人共用底层动力学但各自搭载不同LLM。每台机器人集成LLM策略代理、UCB多臂赌博机用于模式选择，以及基于策略的Double DQN进行动作控制。机器人通过一份包含策略、结果和学习反馈的摘要进行跨LLM通信。LLM每轮生成和调整策略，具体动作则由DQN选择。设置四种不同配置，对每种进行30轮仿真评估。

Result: 最完善的配置（LLM+UCB+DoubleDQN）在所有机器人和回合中均成功到达目标，且完成时间中位数(42 ticks)和P90(73.2 ticks)均最低，较其他配置中位数低25.0-39.1%。

Conclusion: 多异构LLM且决策分层的分布式导航系统在最充分集成策略、模式选择和控制机制时表现最佳。实验为未来无人中心异构机器人团队的设计提供定量经验。

Abstract: This paper addresses navigation by composite heterogeneous robots in a decentralized system when policy reasoning and local control operate at different update levels. In a NetLogo--Python implementation, three robots share motion dynamics but use different LLM backends. Each robot independently combines a large language model (LLM) policy agent, an Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) bandit, and a Double Deep Q-Network (Double DQN) controller; no central LLM generates team actions. LLM inference is confined to round-level policy generation and refinement rather than tick-level action selection. The robots perform cross-LLM communication through a shared round summary containing policies, outcomes, and learning feedback. UCB performs refinement-mode selection, and the policy-conditioned Double DQN performs tick-level action selection from navigation variables, active policy parameters, and the LLM action prior. Each of the four configurations was evaluated over 30 rounds. In the fixed simulation, the complete configuration reached the goal in all 90 correlated robot--round records and achieved the lowest median completion time (42 ticks) and P90 (73.2 ticks); its median was 25.0--39.1\% lower than those of the other configurations. These observations provide descriptive, configuration-level evidence from the evaluated configurations.

</details>


### [164] [LIBERO-RECOVER: Beyond Task Success Towards Failure Recovery in Robotic Manipulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05178)
*Lin Liu,Zhicheng Bao,Lu Zhang,Ziying Song,Wu Yang,Shuai Tao,Wulong Liu,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA或WAM模型在机器人操作上已表现优异，但现有评测忽略了真实场景下的故障恢复能力，该文提出全新大规模故障恢复基准LIBERO-Recover，弥补评测与实际应用的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLA/WAM模型在标准任务上的表现近乎完美，但它们在真实环境中的鲁棒性尚未充分评估。主要动力在于：现有基准只关注任务是否完成，而现实操作常发生意外和故障，机器人需具备恢复能力，这一能力几乎未被评价。

Method: 基于LIBERO基准，作者收集了SOTA模型在真实执行中的失败案例，设计1000+个故障恢复场景，涵盖四种恢复级别：动作重试、动作适应、物体状态恢复和环境恢复，并评测空间理解、物体结构推理、交互理解和拓扑推理等能力。

Result: 提出的LIBERO-Recover是首个大规模故障恢复评测，系统化地对现有SOTA模型在不同恢复场景下进行考核，展示了目前模型在鲁棒性和恢复能力上存在的显著短板。

Conclusion: LIBERO-Recover极大推动了机器人操作系统对“故障后能否恢复”这一真实场景核心能力的关注，标志着机器人评测标准从任务完成率向实用鲁棒性的转变。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) or World Action (WAM) models have recently demonstrated remarkable performance in robotic manipulation. On LIBERO, SOTA method have achieved nearly 100\% success rates, seemingly suggesting that the models are ready for deployment in real world. However, near perfect performance on existing benchmarks can be misleading: success under ideal conditions does not imply real world robustness. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate task completion from predefined initial states, while real world interactions inevitably involve failures such as failed grasps, collisions, and unintended object movements. A robot must therefore not only execute tasks successfully, but also recognize and recover from failures to continue the task. Yet this capability remains largely unmeasured, revealing a critical gap between benchmark performance and real world reliability. To address this gap, we introduce LIBERO-Recover Benchmark, a large scale benchmark for failure recovery in robotic manipulation. Built upon LIBERO, we collect real execution failures from SOTA embodied models and construct 1,000+ scenarios across four recovery levels: (1) Action Retry, (2) Action Adaptation, (3) Object State Recovery, and (4) Environmental Recovery. We evaluate four core capabilities: spatial understanding, object structure reasoning, interaction understanding, and topological reasoning. As the first large-scale benchmark for embodied failure recovery, LIBERO-Recover shifts evaluation from \emph{Can the robot succeed?''} to \emph{Can the robot recover after failure?''}, promoting robust and generalizable embodied agents. The project will be avaible in \textcolor{blue}{https://liulin815.github.io/LIBERO-Recovery/}.

</details>


### [165] [Morphology and actuation as inductive biases in robotic hand manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05206)
*Zalán Tari,Eszter Birtalan,Péter Polcz,Miklós Koller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的分析框架，用以研究不同机械手设计（仿生度与机械复杂性差异）在运动协调与控制难度上的影响，并通过多种强化学习方法对其进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械手设计中，仿生结构和机械复杂性的取舍会明显影响系统的控制难度，但缺乏统一分析和量化的理论工具。本文旨在建立统一的分析框架，为机械手结构优化和控制策略提供理论依据。

Method: 将运动学与驱动结构分开与组合，通过分析任务雅可比矩阵、驱动矩阵及其乘积的条件数，比较两种具代表性的机械手（Shadow Dexterous Hand与仿生手）在关节几何、驱动器与自由度比、耦合架构和驱动分布等四个方面的性能。所有参数都基于标准数字模型。此外，利用多种强化学习算法（PPO、DDPG+HER、TQC+HER）对三种任务进行实验对比。

Result: 高度仿生的机械手设计并无一贯优势：斜向关节轴提升拇指表现但降低其他手指的协调性，复杂肌腱网络改善了长手指的映射质量却极度削弱了拇指的控制效率。理论度量的预测与强化学习实验结果一致。

Conclusion: 机械手的高仿生设计并非绝对优越，应根据具体控制需求与任务特性权衡结构设计方案。统一分析框架可有效支持机械手结构及控制策略的优化。

Abstract: Robotic hands vary widely in anatomical fidelity and mechanical complexity, and these structural choices influence the coordination of joint motions and the difficulty of controlling the system. A unified framework is presented in which the kinematic and actuation stages are analysed separately and in composition, through the conditioning of the task Jacobian, the actuation matrix, and their product. It is applied to two hands representing opposing design philosophies, the Shadow Dexterous Hand and the Anatomically Correct, Biomechatronic Hand, along four morphological aspects: joint axis geometry, actuator-to-DOF ratio, coupling architecture, and authority distribution. All parameters are derived from the hands' canonical digital representations. Anatomical fidelity carries no uniform advantage: oblique axes improve thumb conditioning but leave the long fingers worse conditioned than the orthogonal-axis design, while the branching tendon network improves the effective control mapping at every long finger and worsens it significantly at the thumb, where actuator authority is concentrated on thumb opposition. Predictions derived from these metrics are evaluated against reinforcement learning experiments using PPO, DDPG+HER, and TQC+HER, across three different tasks.

</details>


### [166] [One Word, Different Action: A Real-Robot Benchmark for Language-Conditioned Embodied Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05260)
*Yiwei Liu,Luwei Yang,Shunbo Lei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为“One Word, Different Action”的机器人基准测试，用于评估机器人对自然语言指令变化下行为决策的稳定性与灵敏性，发现机器人在应对多任务约束时表现仍有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然机器人可以根据自然语言指令变更行为，但一个可靠的机器人系统应在任务不变时保持原行为，并能正确响应任务本身改变。现有研究尚缺乏对多约束情境下机器人决策彈性与鲁棒性的深入评估，因此需要新的评测基准。

Method: 作者设计了“One Word, Different Action”真实机器人基准，通过物理决策状态和可执行动作，构建了成对的任务保持/变化指令，联合评测决策不变性与敏感性，并涵盖多约束推理与实际RGB场景解析任务。

Result: 实验证明，现代模型在单一约束变化下表现接近饱和，但面对多个任务约束时，多数模型的性能明显下降，难以有效组合并执行复合指令。

Conclusion: 研究表明，当前的主要挑战已不在于检测单一指令变化，而是如何将多个任务需求可靠地整合为正确的机器人动作决策。

Abstract: Natural-language instruction changes can directly alter robot behavior. A reliable embodied system should preserve its action when the task is unchanged and update it correctly when the task itself changes. We introduce One Word, Different Action, a real-robot benchmark built on physical decision states and executable actions, using task-preserving and task-changing instruction pairs to jointly evaluate Decision Invariance and Decision Sensitivity, with further evaluation under multi-constraint reasoning and real-RGB grounding. Experiments show that modern models are near saturation on single-constraint instruction changes, yet several models degrade noticeably when multiple task constraints must be integrated into one executable decision. These results suggest that the more salient remaining challenge is no longer recognizing an isolated instruction change, but reliably composing multiple task requirements into a correct robot action decision.

</details>


### [167] [TacPAC: Tactile Prediction and Real-Time Action Correction in World-Action Models for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05266)
*Zipei Ma,Xiaofei Wei,Junzhe Jiang,Shunlin Lu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TacPAC方法，用触觉预测进行实时动作纠正，提高了机器人接触丰富操作任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉为主的预测模型无法充分利用操作任务中触觉反馈的关键作用，仅将触觉作为额外视角并不能显著提升效果。需要结合预测与实际触觉输入改进决策。

Method: TacPAC方法将触觉预测转为实时纠正机制：在规划动作块后，缓存与之相关的触觉预测和计划表征，之后用触觉专家模块将实时观测到的触觉图像与缓存对比，纠正尚未执行的动作，且处理开销低。

Result: TacPAC在五项现实机器人任务（包括精密插入、易碎物体操作、物体重新定向、长周期操作）中表现优于基线模型，平均表现由22%提高到64%。

Conclusion: TacPAC有效利用触觉反馈进行实时动作纠正，大幅提升了接触丰富类操作任务的机器人性能。

Abstract: World-action models guide action generation with predicted future observations, but vision-centric predictions miss the local contact cues that decide contact-rich manipulation. However, naively predicting future tactile observations as additional views recovers only a third of the achievable gain in our experiments. This gap reflects a timing mismatch: predictions precede execution, while tactile feedback arrives during it. We introduce TacPAC, which turns tactile prediction into real-time action correction. Once the base model has planned an action chunk, TacPAC caches the predicted contact that plan was conditioned on together with the plan's own representation, and a tactile expert reads each newly observed tactile image against that cache to correct the actions not yet executed. Feedback is thus interpreted against what the plan anticipated rather than in isolation, and one correction is a single pass over that cache, $20.7\times$ cheaper than regenerating the chunk. On five real-robot tasks spanning precision insertion, fragile-object handling, object reorientation, and long-horizon manipulation, TacPAC leads every task and raises the average from 22% for its vision-only base model to 64%. Code is available at https://github.com/LogosRoboticsGroup/TacPAC.

</details>


### [168] [Temporal Tactile Encoding and Compliance for Intent-Aware Robot-to-Human Bimanual Handover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05282)
*Pasquale Marra,Stefano Berti,Gabriele Mario Caddeo,Lorenzo Natale*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种多模态的机器人和人交接物品的方法，结合VLA模型和顺应控制器，通过整合视觉、触觉和本体感受数据提升交接的可靠性与舒适性，实验证明新方法显著优于对比基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在与人进行物品交接时，难以仅靠视觉判断对方是否准备好接物，容易因错误判断造成不安全或不舒适，因此亟需更准确识别接物意图的方法。

Method: 方法上，将人-机交接视为多模态问题，提出VLA模型结合顺应控制器；模型融合RGB视觉、时序触觉反馈和本体感受数据，通过真人演示进行微调，并与无触觉、无顺应两类基线方法进行对比测试。

Result: 主观和客观测评均显示，顺应控制和时序触觉编码两者结合效果最佳，显著提升了交接的可靠性与舒适性，优于单独采用视觉或触觉方案。

Conclusion: 多模态融合加顺应控制为机器人物品交接提供了更可靠的机制，两者互为补充，取得了明显优于常规策略的实验效果，未来代码与数据将开源，为相关领域研究带来推动。

Abstract: Reliable robot-to-human handover requires the robot to infer when the person is ready to receive the object, and release it safely, comfortably, and at the right time. This is challenging because visual observations alone may not disambiguate clear taking intent from accidental contact, weak grasping, wrong-direction forces, or transient interactions. In this work we treat human-robot handover as an intrinsically multimodal problem. Our approach couples a VLA model with a compliance controller that reduces interaction forces during object transfer. We finetune the VLA model with human demonstrations using RGB observation, temporally encoded tactile feedback and proprioception. We evaluate the complete system in a human-subject study against two baselines: one without tactile feedback and one using tactile feedback without compliance control. We hypothesize that combining compliance and temporal tactile encoding yields the most reliable and comfortable handovers, as compliance facilitates physical interaction while tactile history captures sustained taking intent. Performance is measured through objective metrics and an ad-hoc questionnaire. The results show that the two components provide complementary benefits and substantially outperform the baselines. Code and data will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [169] [Human-Human & Human-Robot Interaction Transformer (H2INT) for Robot Navigation in Dense and Uncertain Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05300)
*Ao Shen,Kaixi Chen,Shiwei Liu,Fang Deng,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的人机交互Transformer（H2INT）架构，通过强化学习实现机器人在人群密集环境中的安全导航，显著提升了在不同响应条件和人群密度下的安全性与鲁棒性，并在现实环境中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型导航方法大多假设行人运动不受机器人影响，或者假设所有行人对机器人反应一致，这忽略了人与机器人间真实交互中的不确定性，难以保障机器人在实际复杂人群中的安全和有效导航。

Method: 提出了一种全新的人-人和人-机器人交互Transformer（H2INT），通过两阶段门控Transformer编码人-人以及人-机器人间的关系，结合递归策略网络捕捉时序动态。同时，采用课程学习动态调整人群对机器人的响应程度增强难度，以强化学习方式训练导航策略。

Result: 实验表明，H2INT在多样的人群响应和高密度环境下，相较于主流基线显著提升了导航的安全性与鲁棒性。此外，该方法具备良好的迁移性，无需重新训练即可适应不同人群流动结构。在真实机器人部署中也展现了良好表现。

Conclusion: H2INT实现了对人群响应差异的建模和推断，提升了机器人在人群中的导航能力。分层关系编码和门控更新机制有效支持了复杂交互建模，为机器人安全、自主地在人群中导航提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Safe robot navigation in dense crowds requires reasoning about pedestrian motion and how it may change in response to a robot. However, many learning-based approaches generate pedestrian motion independently of the robot or assume uniform reciprocity, omitting an important source of interaction uncertainty. This paper presents a Human-Human & Human-Robot Interaction Transformer (H2INT), a reinforcement learning framework that retains robot-conditioned changes in pedestrian motion during policy learning while allowing responsiveness to vary across pedestrians. Responsiveness affects the crowd dynamics when the robot is visible but is not supplied as a policy input; the policy must instead infer its consequences from robot-centered relative positions. A two-stage gated Transformer progressively encodes human-human and human-robot relations, while a recurrent policy captures their temporal evolution. A curriculum gradually reduces pedestrian responsiveness to increase interaction difficulty. Simulation experiments demonstrate improved navigation safety and robustness over representative baselines across response conditions and crowd densities, and show transfer without retraining to structurally distinct crowd-flow layouts. Ablations support the hierarchical relational encoding and gated updates. Real-robot deployment further verifies that the learned policy can operate with sparse observations in a physical environment.

</details>


### [170] [RoboSPA: Can VLA Models Go Beyond Simple Scenes and Short-Horizon Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05324)
*Zhenxuan Fan,Bo Zhang,Yutong Lin,Yuqian Yuan,Juekai Lin,Liang Liang,Zhuoyi Huang,Wenqiao Zhang,Juncheng Li,Siliang Tang,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了RoboSPA数据集与基准，用于系统诊断VLA模型在复杂空间推理和长期过程规划方面的能力。实验结果显示主流系统在这些维度仍有显著挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA数据集和基准多以预定义简单设置来衡量模型表现，缺乏对机器人模型面对逐渐增长的空间歧义与过程复杂性的能力诊断。迫切需要更细致、更具挑战的数据集来推动VLA模型在真实应用中推理、规划等能力的发展。

Method: RoboSPA数据集专注于精细空间推理和长程过程规划两个核心维度，涵盖10类任务、56个基础任务，每个任务包含5个难度级别，总计280个变体，采集了527K条机器人操作轨迹。引入了超过简单成功率的诊断型评估指标。

Result: 在代表性VLA模型上的实验证明：当前系统在复杂空间关系、精细低阶执行以及需较强记忆的长序列规划任务中依然表现有限，难以很好地应对高难度推理和规划。

Conclusion: RoboSPA建立了用于诊断且极具挑战性的基准，有望推动更强大、更可靠、更通用的具身智能体模型的发展。数据和代码已开源，便于后续社区研究利用。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promising progress in language-conditioned robotic manipulation. However, existing datasets and benchmarks mainly evaluate task completion under predefined settings, offering limited insight into model reasoning under increasing spatial and procedural complexity. We introduce \textbf{RoboSPA} (\textbf{Robo}t \textbf{S}patial-\textbf{P}rocedural \textbf{A}ssessment), a large-scale robotic manipulation dataset and benchmark for diagnosing embodied reasoning in VLA models. \texttt{RoboSPA} focuses on two core dimensions, Fine-Grained Spatial Reasoning and Long-Horizon Procedural Planning, covering 10 task categories and 56 base tasks. Each task is instantiated across five difficulty levels, yielding 280 variants with increasing spatial ambiguity and procedural complexity. We collect 527K trajectories across multiple embodiments and diverse scenes. Beyond binary success rate, \texttt{RoboSPA} introduces diagnostic metrics for more detailed evaluation. Experiments on representative VLA models show that current systems still struggle with complex spatial relations, precise low-level execution, and memory-intensive planning. These results establish \texttt{RoboSPA} as a challenging diagnostic benchmark for developing more capable, reliable, and generalizable embodied agents. Our data and code are available at https://github.com/fanzhenxuan/RoboSPA.

</details>


### [171] [FIRE-LIVWO: Robust LiDAR-Inertial-Visual-Wheel Odometry via Failure-Immune mmWave Radar Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05325)
*Kun Hu,Menggang Li,Kaidi Wu,Zhiwen Jin,Yingjie Zhao,Chaoquan Tang,Eryi Hu,Gongbo Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为FIRE-LIVWO的多传感器里程计融合方法，利用毫米波雷达、LiDAR、视觉与轮式数据实现复杂煤矿环境下稳健定位；通过特征自适应权重调整，大幅提升了系统应对烟尘与几何退化环境的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型地下煤矿等复杂、恶劣环境中，由于烟尘严重导致视觉信息丢失、LiDAR特征退化以及长而自相似的通道带来几何退化，常规SLAM方法定位精度和鲁棒性不足，亟须突破和创新。

Method: 提出FIRE-LIVWO框架，基于迭代误差状态卡尔曼滤波（IESKF）紧耦合融合毫米波雷达、LiDAR、视觉和轮式数据；在统一VoxelMap下联合构建点云和视觉残差；在烟尘环境中利用毫米波雷达穿透特性和速度约束维持可观性，在几何退化通道中引入轮式非完整约束和实时杆臂补偿；创新贡献在于在线检测退化并自适应调整多模态数据融合权重。

Result: 在真实煤矿场景中进行实验，FIRE-LIVWO能准确识别失效边界，实现动态模态切换，显著提升定位的精度和鲁棒性，平均定位误差5.677米，超越现有方法。

Conclusion: FIRE-LIVWO为极端地下环境下鲁棒的多传感器SLAM提供创新方案，提高了准确性和可靠性，并已开源以惠及机器人社区。

Abstract: Achieving robust SLAM in large-scale underground coal mines with complex structures and severe degeneracies remains highly challenging. Dense smoke and dust cause substantial loss of visual information and degrade LiDAR point-cloud features, while long, self-similar corridors induce geometric degeneration, leading to pronounced odometry drift. To address these issues, we propose FIRE-LIVWO: Failure-Immune mmWave Radar-Enhanced LiDAR-Inertial-Visual-Wheel Odometry, a tightly coupled multi-modal odometry framework based on an iterated error-state Kalman filter (IESKF). The framework fuses 4D mmWave radar, LiDAR, and visual features within a unified VoxelMap and jointly constructs LiDAR-radar point-to-plane residuals and sparse visual photometric residuals. In smoke-filled environments, we exploit the strong penetration of 4D mmWave radar and introduce pointwise Doppler velocity constraints to preserve state observability. In geometrically degenerate corridors, we tightly couple wheel odometry using non-holonomic constraints (NHC) and online lever-arm compensation to reduce drift. Our central contribution is a degeneration detection and adaptive fusion model switching strategy grounded in geometric and visual observability analysis, which quantifies observability online and dynamically adjusts modality weights. Real-world experiments in underground coal mines demonstrate that FIRE-LIVWO accurately identifies failure boundaries, enabling reliable modality switching under extreme conditions. Compared with baselines, it achieves superior accuracy and robustness (average localization error of 5.677m). We open source our code on Github to benefit the robotics community.

</details>


### [172] [Adaptation Needs in Robotic Systems: Assessing Behavior Trees and Their Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05331)
*Mehran Rostamnia,Gianluca Filippone,Ricardo Caldas,Patrizio Pelliccione*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨行为树（BTs）在适应现代机器人系统变化中的能力和局限，并梳理与总结了现有的行为树增强方法和应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人应用于动态、不确定和开放环境，传统设计假设难以持续成立，需要研究控制架构对适应性的支持。行为树广泛应用于机器人控制系统，其可读性、模块化和反应性优点明确，因此研究其是否足以支持系统适应性是核心问题。

Method: 采用文献研究和实证验证。首先从文献中归纳出六类（知识、感知、执行、系统、任务、环境）机器人适应性需求，然后分析经典行为树的适用性和局限，并系统梳理已有的四大类增强行为树方法：生成、扩展、进化、细化。

Result: 经典行为树在面对运行时结构重组、不确定性推理、任务重解释、学习等适应性需求，以及与外部知识和规划机制整合时表现不足。多种增强型行为树方法可以部分解决，但仍各有局限。

Conclusion: 提供了适应性需求与行为树能力对照的指导意见，指出了经典与增强行为树各自适用的场景，并总结了当前适应性机器人控制体系仍面临的挑战。

Abstract: Robotic systems increasingly operate in dynamic, uncertain, and open-ended environments, where design-time assumptions may no longer hold, and adaptation becomes necessary to maintain effective and safe operation. Behavior Trees (BTs) are widely used in robotic control architectures due to their modularity, readability, and reactivity. This raises a central question: are BTs sufficient to meet the adaptation needs of modern robotic systems?
  This paper investigates this question through a literature-driven study complemented by empirical validation. First, we derive a classification of robotic adaptation needs from the literature, organizing them into six categories: Knowledge, Perception, Actuation, System, Mission, and Environment. Then, we analyze the capabilities and limitations of classical BTs with respect to these needs. Then, we characterize BT-based approaches for adaptation from the existing literature and organize them into four primary families, i.e., generation, extension, evolution, and refinement, including approaches that combine multiple families.
  Our analysis shows that the modularity, flexibility, and reactivity of classical BTs are insufficient for adaptation needs involving runtime restructuring, reasoning under uncertainty, mission reinterpretation, learning, or integration with external knowledge and planning mechanisms. Enhanced BT approaches address several of these limitations, but to different extents and often with limitations of their own. Our findings relate adaptation needs to both the capabilities and limitations of classical and enhanced BTs, providing guidance on when classical BTs are sufficient, when enhanced mechanisms are needed, and which challenges remain or emerge for adaptive robotic control architectures.

</details>


### [173] [Development of a Humanoid Robot Prototype for Multimodal Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05361)
*Thang Tran Viet,Thanh Nguyen Canh,Huy Uong Gia,Phuc Dinh Van,Son Tran Duc,Ngoc Minh Do,Xiem HoangVan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种用于人机交互（HRI）任务的多自由度仿人机器人原型平台，并集成了多个AI模块，实现了优秀的人机协作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人机交互对平台的灵活性和AI集成度提出更高要求，而现有平台多受限于定制化程度高、拓展性弱，限制了AI模块在真实环境中的测试与部署。为此，作者研制了一个高可扩展性、便于AI集成的仿人机器人平台。

Method: 该系统采用12自由度双臂与2自由度头部，配备可表达面部情感的LCD屏。总线硬件由定制控制板管理，并借助Jetson模块实现AI实时处理。集成了手势识别（MediaPipe Pose+LSTM）、基于YOLO的三维目标检测、以LLM为核心的语音命令识别与语义解析等三大AI功能模块。

Result: 机器人在定位精度实验中，操作误差平均约1.83 cm。在实际任务测试中，总体完成准确率超过90%，其中手势识别准确率96%，语音识别92%，展现了很强的多模态交互与任务执行能力。

Conclusion: 该平台为HRI研究和原型设计提供了一套可复现、便于集成AI模块的基础设施，有助于推动人机协作领域，实验结果验证了系统的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Human-robot interaction (HRI) enables intuitive and intelligent collaboration between humans and robots in real-world environments. This paper introduces a humanoid robot prototype designed as a flexible testbed for developing and integrating artificial intelligence (AI) modules in HRI tasks. The system features a 12 degree-of-freedom (DOFs) dual-arm mechanism and a 2 DOFs head with an expressive LCD screen to express facial emotions. All hardware components are controlled by a custom-designed controller board with real-time AI processing supported by an onboard Jetson module. The system incorporates three AI modules: (1) gesture recognition using MediaPipe Pose and an LSTM classifier, (2) object detection with YOLO and 3D localization, and (3) voice-command processing through speech recognition and large language model(LLM)-based semantic parsing. The platform is validated through experiments on positioning accuracy, with results showing average manipulation errors of approximately 1.83 cm. To demonstrate its versatility, experimental results show over 90% task accuracy, with gesture recognition reaching 96%, speech recognition reaching 92%. The results confirm the effectiveness of the proposed system as a reproducible and accessible humanoid platform for research and prototyping in HRI.

</details>


### [174] [Towards Neuro-Symbolic Procedural Reasoning for Long-Horizon Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05369)
*Vivek Chavan,Yahuan Shi,Oliver Heimann,Kevin Haninger,Jörg Krüger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号框架，将视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型与显式任务图和多模态程序记忆结合，用于解决长时序任务中的困难，并通过人类演示引导模型更好地完成复杂操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在执行短时操作上表现良好，但在需要持续状态跟踪、依赖推理、条件决策和稳健环境对应的长时任务中，表现较为脆弱。为提升VLA模型在实际复杂场景中处理长时任务的鲁棒性和智能化水平，需要引入结构化的任务编码和有效的任务状态维持机制。

Method: 文章提出神经符号混合架构，将神经网络（负责VLA控制）与符号任务图和多模态记忆（用于存储关键任务状态、已完成及待完成动作、文本与视觉信息）结合，并借助人类演示（如注视点）为学习和推理提供指引。通过人工标注伪注视轨迹，辅助VLA模型在两个长时序操作领域进行微调和推理，包括工作区清理和外科手术器械操作。

Result: 在多个长时序操控任务上，系统在对象与目标选取、子任务完成度、执行顺序一致性、整体任务完成情况和错误类型上均有系统评估，显示神经符号架构和演示指导对提升模型稳定性和任务表现具有积极作用。

Conclusion: 结构化的符号推理与基于人类演示的视觉引导相辅相成，可有效提升长时序VLA操控任务的可靠性，是实现复杂机器人操作智能化的重要方向。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can execute short manipulation skills, but remain brittle in long-horizon procedures requiring persistent task state, dependency-aware reasoning, conditional decisions, and reliable grounding. We investigate a neuro-symbolic framework that combines learned VLA control with explicit task graphs and multimodal procedural memory. Task graphs encode action dependencies, valid transitions, and branch conditions, while memory maintains the active step, completed actions, textual context, and task-relevant visual evidence. Together, these structures guide object selection, destination grounding, subgoal dispatch, and verification of expected state transitions. Human demonstrations provide additional spatial and temporal guidance through gaze or saliency cues. To isolate their effect on policy learning, our initial study bypasses cross-view gaze transfer and directly annotates pseudo-gaze in robot-view teleoperation videos. The resulting guidance is used during VLA fine-tuning and inference. We study two long-horizon manipulation domains, workspace clearing and surgical-instrument handling, which require ordered execution, visually grounded decisions, and conditional branching. We evaluate correct-object and destination selection, subtask completion, task progress, step-order consistency, complete-task success, and procedural or execution mistakes. This work positions structured symbolic reasoning and demonstration-derived visual guidance as complementary mechanisms for reliable long-horizon VLA manipulation.

</details>


### [175] [What Matters, When? Diagnosing and Improving Conditional Visual Grounding in Visuomotor Imitation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05376)
*Vivek Chavan,Pengtao Xie,Yahuan Shi,Oliver Heimann,Kevin Haninger,Jörg Krüger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉干扰对视觉-运动模仿政策的影响，发现视觉干扰物会导致操作失败，并提出了多种干预方法以提升策略鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然视觉-运动模仿策略在训练分布内下表现良好，但在遇到看似相似但实际不同的干扰物时，往往会出现目标选择错误、操作失败。理解和提升这类策略在复杂实际环境中的泛化能力成为亟需解决的问题。

Method: 作者通过引入颜色和形状可控的干扰物与容器，系统性分析了不同时期（如抓取阶段或放置阶段）下的失败模式，进一步检验了干扰增强、阶段相关注意力正则化及基于外观的视觉提示等干预方法的效果。实验不仅涵盖仿真，还拓展到实际硬件机器人上。

Result: 实验显示，视觉干扰的敏感性与干扰类型和操作阶段相关。针对性的干预措施可以显著提升策略在分心物存在下对目标对象的选择准确性和整体鲁棒性，并且适用于多种策略学习框架。

Conclusion: 视觉干扰不仅会导致目标或位置选择错误，进而影响操作任务表现，但不影响底层操作能力。通过针对目标选择问题设计干预，可以大幅恢复视觉-运动策略的性能，有助于提升其在实际复杂环境下的应用前景。

Abstract: Visuomotor imitation policies can achieve high performance under in-distribution visual conditions yet fail when visually similar objects or receptacles are introduced. We study this behavior as a problem of conditional visual grounding: the visual target required for successful control changes with the manipulation phase and, in more complex tasks, with the observed task state. Using Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT), we systematically introduce distractor objects and receptacles with controlled color and shape similarity and localize failures to picking and placement. We find that distractor sensitivity is specific to both the type of visual similarity and the manipulation stage. Guided by this diagnosis, we evaluate distractor augmentation, phase-dependent attention regularization, and appearance-based visual prompting as complementary interventions for improving target selection while preserving spatial information required for control. These interventions substantially improve robustness in simulation and on a physical UR3e. We further examine the same failure pattern in a pretrained vision-language-action policy on a state-conditioned instrument-handling task, where the observed state of a medical instrument determines the correct destination. Together, the results show that visual distractors can cause incorrect object or destination selection even when the underlying manipulation skill remains intact, and that explicitly improving target selection can substantially recover performance across distinct visuomotor policy-learning regimes.

</details>


### [176] [Same Trajectory, Contradictory Rewards (ROBORMBENCH): Paraphrase Fragility in Vision Language Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05401)
*Wonje Jeung,Sangyeon Yoon,Hyesoo Hong,Yoonjun Cho,Dongjae Jeon,Bumjun Kim,Jean Oh,Youngjae Yu,Albert No*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文发现视觉语言模型（VLM）在作为机器人学习奖励函数时对同义复述的鲁棒性很差，为更好地评估这一问题，作者构建了一个包含真实机器人轨迹和大量同义目标描述的基准（ROBORMBENCH），并发现现有VLM对指令的措辞变化极其敏感，专门用轨迹-监督训练的奖励模型则稳定得多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型被广泛应用于机器人任务奖励建模，但模型并未被系统性评估是否对语义等价、措辞不同的目标描述具有一致判断（即同义鲁棒性），这一缺陷会直接影响机器人的可靠性和普适性。

Method: 作者提出了ROBORMBENCH基准，涵盖2390个真实机器人轨迹、真实进展标注，以及21673个经验证的同义复述，涵盖词汇、句法和任务变化。基于该基准，系统评估了多种主流VLM模型在各种措辞变换下的奖励输出稳定性，并与专门用轨迹-监督训练的奖励模型对比。

Result: 主流专有和开源的VLM在目标指令同义改写下输出波动剧烈，且该不稳定性随着改写强度增加变本加厉，提升模型规模或引入显性推理机制也不能有效改善，反而用真实轨迹监督训练的奖励模型性能更稳健。

Conclusion: 语义等价表述的一致性（即复述鲁棒性）是VLM奖励函数在机器人应用中的关键要求，目前主流方法普遍不满足，需针对该问题优化或采用更贴合任务的监督训练。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used as reward functions for robotic learning, but this role requires paraphrase invariance: the same trajectory should receive the same reward under semantically equivalent goal descriptions. We show that current VLM reward models often violate this property. Paraphrasing the instruction alone can substantially change predicted progress scores, and can even flip identical robot behavior between failure and success. To measure this failure mode, we introduce ROBORMBENCH, a benchmark with 2,390 real-robot trajectories, ground-truth progress labels, and 21,673 verified paraphrases spanning lexical, syntactic, and action-goal rewrites. Across proprietary and open-source VLMs, paraphrase-induced instability is widespread and severe, grows under more divergent rewrites, and is not reliably reduced by scale or explicit reasoning. Dedicated reward models trained with trajectory-grounded supervision are substantially more stable. These results show that paraphrase robustness is a core requirement for reliable VLM-based reward modeling in robotics.

</details>
